# The Tide Mother — Video Prompts for Grok Imagine (3–6 s clips)

*424 beats, 31:02 total. Generate each beat as a **6-second** Grok video from its own image, then trim it to the length shown (the narrated prologue beats are generated at 10 or 15 s). Each beat has at most **one speaking character**, and their voice line is repeated in every beat so the voice stays the same.*

**How to use in Grok Imagine**
- Upload the beat's image, choose **Make video**, set **16:9** and **6 s**, and paste the prompt. Trim to the length shown.
- Put the beats back to back in number order.
- **Narration is only in the prologue.** Record it separately (Grok or TTS) with the narrator voice and lay it over the prologue beats in the edit.
